## Choose between Invoicing solutions
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution>

- [parameter] Who should use it: PayPal Invoicing (Dashboard)=Businesses that want a no-code solution and manually create invoices from the PayPal dashboard <br />Ideal for low to moderate invoice volume; Invoicing REST API=Businesses that need automated invoicing, higher invoice volumes, or integration with backend systems such as ERP, CRM, or SaaS platforms — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution#comparision
- [parameter] How invoices are created: PayPal Invoicing (Dashboard)=Manually in the PayPal dashboard; Invoicing REST API=Programmatically via REST API calls from your backend — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution#comparision
- [parameter] Technical requirements: PayPal Invoicing (Dashboard)=No development required; Invoicing REST API=Requires backend development and REST API integration <br />Uses OAuth 2.0 authentication — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution#comparision
- [parameter] Automation: PayPal Invoicing (Dashboard)=Limited to dashboard-based workflows; Invoicing REST API=Full automation of invoice creation, sending, reminders, and reconciliation — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution#comparision
- [parameter] Invoice lifecycle management: PayPal Invoicing (Dashboard)=Manage invoices in the PayPal dashboard (status tracking, reminders, refunds, offline payments); Invoicing REST API=Manage invoices via API and webhooks <br />Invoices are also visible in the PayPal dashboard for operational support — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution#comparision
- [parameter] Payer experience: PayPal Invoicing (Dashboard)=PayPal-managed payment workflow with multiple payment methods such as PayPal, debit and credit cards, digital wallets, and eligible local payment methods; Invoicing REST API=PayPal-managed payment workflow with multiple payment methods such as PayPal, debit and credit cards, digital wallets, and eligible local payment methods — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution#comparision
- [definition] Choose between Invoicing solutions — PayPal Invoicing supports two ways to create and manage invoices: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution#choose-between-invoicing-solutions
- [concept] PayPal Invoicing supports two ways to create and manage invoices: manual creation via the Dashboard or programmatic creation via the REST API. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution
- [fact] The PayPal Invoicing (Dashboard) option allows users to create, send, and manage invoices directly from their PayPal business account without writing code. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution
- [concept] The Invoicing REST API enables programmatic creation, updating, sending, and management of invoices from backend systems. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution
- [fact] All invoices are accessible in the PayPal dashboard for tracking and management regardless of whether they were created manually or programmatically. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution
- [idea] Users should choose between manual Dashboard creation or programmatic API integration based on their need for automation versus direct control. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/choose-solution

## Core concepts
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts>

- [parameter] `DRAFT`: The invoice exists, but the customer hasn't been notified. You can edit all fields freely. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `SENT`: The invoice is delivered and payable. The customer is notified unless you suppress notification. You can't return a sent invoice to `DRAFT`. If the invoice has a future issue date, PayPal holds it and sends it automatically on that date. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `PARTIALLY_PAID`: A partial payment has been recorded. The balance decreases, but the invoice stays open. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `PAID`: The full amount was paid through PayPal's hosted page. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `MARKED_AS_PAID`: The full amount was recorded manually via the Record External Payment endpoint. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `REFUNDED`: The invoice was fully refunded through PayPal. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `MARKED_AS_REFUNDED`: A full refund was recorded manually. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED`: A partial refund was issued. The balance increases accordingly. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `CANCELLED`: The invoice was canceled after being sent. Canceled invoices are retained for audit purposes and remain queryable. To remove a draft invoice, delete it instead. You can't cancel a draft. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice-lifecycle
- [parameter] `QUANTITY`: Best for=Discrete, countable items; Example=2 widgets at \$50 each — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#billing-types
- [parameter] `HOURS`: Best for=Time-based services; Example=12 hours \$150/hour — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#billing-types
- [parameter] `AMOUNT`: Best for=Flat-rate charges with no quantity breakdown; Example=Consultation: \$500 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#billing-types
- [definition] Core concepts — The PayPal Invoicing API helps you create, send, and track invoices programmatically. This page describes the core resources, the invoice lifecycle, and the authentication model. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#core-concepts
- [definition] Core resources — The API centers on the following three primary resources: invoices, templates, and payment records. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#core-resources
- [definition] Invoice — An invoice is a hosted payment request that PayPal delivers to your customer and manages through different states over its lifecycle. Unlike a static PDF, an invoice is a dynamic object that moves through distinct states from creation to payment, refund, or cancellation. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#invoice
- [definition] Templates — A template is a reusable invoice configuration that pre-populates common fields, including merchant details, default line items, payment terms, tax settings, and branding. Templates decouple invoice design from invoice creation. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#templates
- [definition] Payment and refund records — The API distinguishes between two types of payment recording. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#payment-and-refund-records
- [definition] Billing types — Each line item on an invoice uses one of three unit-of-measure types. A single invoice can combine all three. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#billing-types
- [definition] External system integration — If you manage invoices in an external system, such as an ERP or accounting platform, two fields let you bridge it with your system. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#external-system-integration
- [definition] Authentication — The Invoicing API uses OAuth 2.0. Every request requires an access token in the `Authorization` header. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts#authentication
- [concept] The PayPal Invoicing API centers on three primary resources: invoices, templates, and payment records. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [concept] An invoice is a dynamic object that moves through distinct states from creation to payment, refund, or cancellation, unlike a static PDF. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [fact] Customers can access the secure payment page via a PayPal-issued email link or a QR code generated by the merchant. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [concept] A template is a reusable configuration that pre-populates fields like merchant details, tax settings, and branding to decouple design from creation. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [fact] You can create up to 50 templates, but standard system templates provided by PayPal cannot be deleted. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [idea] Templates allow you to override specific values in the invoice request while inheriting defaults like merchant name and logo. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [fact] Once an invoice is created from a template, it becomes independent; updating the template does not affect existing invoices. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [concept] The API supports automatic payment recording for PayPal-hosted payments and manual recording for external channels like checks or bank transfers. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [actionable] Call the Record External Payment endpoint to document receipts for payments received outside PayPal, such as cash or wire transfers. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [fact] Invoices remain in the DRAFT state until explicitly sent to prevent customer notification of incomplete requests. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [problem] You cannot unsend an invoice once it has been sent, though you can cancel it for audit purposes. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [fact] Canceled invoices are retained rather than deleted to support compliance and dispute resolution. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [concept] Each line item uses one of three unit-of-measure types, and a single invoice can combine all three billing types. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [actionable] Use the detail.invoice_number field to store your external system's unique invoice ID, such as NETSUITE-INV-78432. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [actionable] Use the detail.reference field to cross-reference order or PO numbers visible to customers on the payment page. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [fact] The Invoicing API uses OAuth 2.0, requiring an access token in the Authorization header for every request. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [fact] The https://api.paypal.com/v1/invoicing:* scope grants full access to invoicing operations. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [problem] Access tokens expire after approximately 9 hours and there is no refresh token available. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [idea] Cache the access token with its expiration timestamp and proactively request a new one before it expires rather than waiting for a 401 response. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [actionable] Include the PayPal-Auth-Assertion header on requests when acting on behalf of multiple merchants to identify the specific merchant context. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [actionable] Use the PayPal-Request-Id header to ensure idempotency, allowing retries on network errors without creating duplicate invoices. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts
- [problem] PayPal invalidates tokens when client credentials are revoked, API scope grants are removed, or the merchant account status changes. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/core-concepts

- [parameter] `401`: Error code=`AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE`; Description=Invalid or missing access token. The token may have expired.; Retryable=Yes, after refreshing the token. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [parameter] `403`: Error code=`AUTHORIZATION_FAILURE`; Description=Valid token, but insufficient permissions for this operation.; Retryable=No — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [parameter] `404`: Error code=`NOT_FOUND`; Description=Invoice, template, or resource doesn't exist.; Retryable=No — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [parameter] `409`: Error code=`CONFLICT`; Description=Operation conflicts with the current resource state, for example, invoice already sent or recipient changed twice in 72 hours.; Retryable=No — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [parameter] `422`: Error code=`UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY`; Description=Request is well-formed but violates business rules, for example, payment amount exceeds invoice total.; Retryable=No — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [parameter] `429`: Error code=`RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`; Description=Too many requests in the time window.; Retryable=Yes, with exponential backoff. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [parameter] `500`: Error code=`INTERNAL_ERROR`; Description=Unexpected server error.; Retryable=Yes, with exponential backoff. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [parameter] `502`: Error code=`TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE`; Description=Service temporarily unavailable.; Retryable=Yes, with exponential backoff. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [parameter] `503`: Error code=`SYSTEM_ERROR`; Description=Scheduled maintenance or service degradation.; Retryable=Yes, with exponential backoff. Check the status page. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [definition] Errors — All Invoicing API error responses follow a consistent structure. This guide covers error codes, retry guidance, and client handling patterns. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#errors
- [definition] Canonical error object — All error responses follow this structure. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#canonical-error-object
- [definition] Error taxonomy — The following table lists all error codes, their HTTP status, and whether the request can be retried. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#error-taxonomy
- [definition] Common error scenarios — The following scenarios show common errors and how to resolve them. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#common-error-scenarios
- [definition] Record a payment on a PayPal-processed invoice — If you try to record a payment on an invoice that PayPal has already processed, you receive the following error. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#record-a-payment-on-a-paypal-processed-invoice
- [definition] Client handling guidance — The following guidance covers how to handle different error types in your integration. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#client-handling-guidance
- [definition] Retryable errors — For `5xx` and `429` errors, implement exponential backoff with jitter. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#retryable-errors
- [definition] Non-retryable errors — For `4xx` errors other than `401` and `429`, return the error to the user with the `debug_id`. Don't retry. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#non-retryable-errors
- [definition] Authentication errors — For `401` errors, refresh the access token and retry the request once. If the second attempt also returns `401`, return the error to the user. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/errors#authentication-errors

## Invoice object
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object>

- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#simple
- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#complex
- [parameter] `id`: Type=string; Required=Read-only; Description=The unique PayPal-generated invoice ID (`INV2-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`). — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `status`: Type=string; Required=Read-only; Description=The current status of the invoice. Values: `DRAFT`, `SENT`, `SCHEDULED`, `PAID`, `MARKED_AS_PAID`, `CANCELLED`, `REFUNDED`, `PARTIALLY_PAID`, `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED`, `MARKED_AS_REFUNDED`, `UNPAID`, `PAYMENT_PENDING`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `detail`: Type=object; Required=Required; Description=Core invoice details including invoice number, date, currency, and payment terms. See [**detail**](#detail) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `invoicer`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The business information of the invoice sender. See [**invoicer**](#invoicer) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `primary_recipients`: Type=array; Required=Optional; Description=The billing and shipping information for the invoice recipients. Up to 100 recipients. See [**primary\_recipients**](#primary_recipients) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `additional_recipients`: Type=array; Required=Optional; Description=An array of CC email addresses to receive invoice notifications. Up to 100 addresses. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `items`: Type=array; Required=Optional; Description=The line items on the invoice. Up to 100 items. See [**items**](#items) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `configuration`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=Invoice configuration options including partial payment, tips, and tax settings. See [**configuration**](#configuration) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `amount`: Type=object; Required=Read-only; Description=The computed invoice amount summary including totals, tax, discounts, and shipping. See [**amount**](#amount) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `due_amount`: Type=object; Required=Read-only; Description=The amount outstanding on the invoice. Contains `currency_code` and `value`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `gratuity`: Type=object; Required=Read-only; Description=The tip amount added by the payer. Contains `currency_code` and `value`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `payments`: Type=object; Required=Read-only; Description=Payments recorded against the invoice. See [**payments**](#payments) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `refunds`: Type=object; Required=Read-only; Description=Refunds recorded against the invoice. See [**refunds**](#refunds) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `links`: Type=array; Required=Read-only; Description=HATEOAS links for available actions on the invoice, for example, `send`, `replace`, `delete`, `qr-code`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [parameter] `invoice_number`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=A merchant-defined invoice number. Max 25 characters. If omitted, PayPal auto-generates one. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `invoice_date`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The invoice date in `YYYY-MM-DD` format. Defaults to the date the invoice is created. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `currency_code`: Type=string; Required=Required; Description=The three-character ISO-4217 currency code, for example, `USD`, `GBP`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `reference`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=A merchant-defined reference identifier, for example, a purchase order number. Max 120 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `payment_term`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The payment due terms. Contains `term_type`, for example, `NET_10`, `NET_30`, `DUE_ON_DATE_SPECIFIED`, and `due_date`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `note`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=A note to the recipient. Visible on the invoice. Max 4,000 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `terms_and_conditions`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The terms and conditions of the invoice. Max 4,000 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `memo`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=A memo for internal use. Not visible to the recipient. Max 500 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `attachments`: Type=array; Required=Optional; Description=File references to attach to the invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `metadata`: Type=object; Required=Read-only; Description=Audit data including `create_time`, `last_update_time`, `recipient_view_url`, and `invoicer_view_url`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [parameter] `email_address`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The invoicer's email address. Must match the email of the PayPal business account associated with the access token. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [parameter] `name`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The invoicer's name. Contains `given_name` and `surname`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [parameter] `address`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The invoicer's address. Contains `address_line_1`, `address_line_2`, `admin_area_1`, `admin_area_2`, `postal_code`, and `country_code`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [parameter] `phones`: Type=array; Required=Optional; Description=The invoicer's phone numbers. Each entry contains `country_code`, `national_number`, and `phone_type`, for example, `MOBILE`, `HOME`, `WORK`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [parameter] `website`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The invoicer's website URL. Max 2,048 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [parameter] `tax_id`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The invoicer's tax ID. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [parameter] `logo_url`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The URL of a logo to display on the invoice. Must be HTTPS. Max 2,000 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [parameter] `additional_notes`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=Additional notes about the invoicer. Max 40 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [parameter] `email_address`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The recipient's email address. PayPal sends the invoice notification to this address. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#billing-info
- [parameter] `name`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The recipient's name. Contains `given_name` and `surname`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#billing-info
- [parameter] `address`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The recipient's billing address. Same structure as `invoicer.address`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#billing-info
- [parameter] `phones`: Type=array; Required=Optional; Description=The recipient's phone numbers. Same structure as `invoicer.phones`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#billing-info
- [parameter] `additional_info_value`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=A free-text field for additional recipient information, for example, account number. Max 40 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#billing-info
- [parameter] `name`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The recipient's name for shipping. Contains `given_name` and `surname`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#shipping-info
- [parameter] `address`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The shipping destination address. Same structure as `invoicer.address`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#shipping-info
- [parameter] `name`: Type=string; Required=Required; Description=The item name. Max 200 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [parameter] `description`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=A description of the item. Max 1,000 characters. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [parameter] `quantity`: Type=string; Required=Required; Description=The quantity of the item. Supports decimals, for example, `"2.5"`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [parameter] `unit_amount`: Type=object; Required=Required; Description=The price per unit. Contains `currency_code` and `value`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [parameter] `tax`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=Tax applied to this item. Contains `name`, `percent`, and a computed `amount`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [parameter] `discount`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=Discount applied to this item. Contains either `percent` or a fixed `amount`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [parameter] `item_date`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The date the item or service was provided. Format: `YYYY-MM-DD`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [parameter] `unit_of_measure`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The unit of measure for the item. Values: `QUANTITY`, `HOURS`, `AMOUNT`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [parameter] `partial_payment`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=Partial payment settings. Contains `allow_partial_payment` and `minimum_amount_due`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#configuration
- [parameter] `allow_tip`: Type=boolean; Required=Optional; Description=If `true`, the payer can add a tip when paying. Default: `false`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#configuration
- [parameter] `tax_calculated_after_discount`: Type=boolean; Required=Optional; Description=If `true`, tax is calculated on the post-discount amount. Default: `false`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#configuration
- [parameter] `tax_inclusive`: Type=boolean; Required=Optional; Description=If `true`, the unit amounts already include tax. Default: `false`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#configuration
- [parameter] `template_id`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The ID of a saved template to associate with this invoice. Doesn't auto-populate template data. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#configuration
- [parameter] `currency_code`: Type=string; Description=The invoice currency. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [parameter] `value`: Type=string; Description=The total invoice amount. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [parameter] `breakdown`: Type=object; Description=A detailed breakdown of the total. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [parameter] `breakdown.item_total`: Type=object; Description=The subtotal of all line items before tax or discount. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [parameter] `breakdown.discount`: Type=object; Description=The total discount applied. Contains `item_discount` and `invoice_discount`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [parameter] `breakdown.tax_total`: Type=object; Description=The total tax applied across all items and shipping. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [parameter] `breakdown.shipping`: Type=object; Description=The shipping amount and any associated tax. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [parameter] `breakdown.custom`: Type=object; Description=A custom line item, for example, packing charges. Contains `label` and `amount`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [parameter] `paid_amount`: Type=object; Description=The total amount paid so far. Contains `currency_code` and `value`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#payments
- [parameter] `transactions`: Type=array; Description=An array of individual payment records. Each contains `payment_id`, `method`, `payment_date`, `note`, and `amount`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#payments
- [parameter] `refund_amount`: Type=object; Description=The total amount refunded so far. Contains `currency_code` and `value`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#refunds
- [parameter] `transactions`: Type=array; Description=An array of individual refund records. Each contains `refund_id`, `method`, `refund_date`, and `amount`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#refunds
- [definition] Invoice object — An invoice is a payment request you send to a customer for goods or services delivered. In the PayPal Invoicing API, invoices flow through a lifecycle: created as `DRAFT`, sent to customers, tracked through payment states (`UNPAID`, `PARTIALLY_PAID`, `PAID`), and optionally refunded. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoice-object
- [definition] Sample invoice objects — The following examples show a simple and complex invoice object. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#sample-invoice-objects
- [definition] Object schema — The following tables document the top-level fields in the invoice object, along with the fields in each nested object. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#object-schema
- [definition] detail — This is core invoice metadata that is required when creating an invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#detail
- [definition] invoicer — The sender's business information displayed on the invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#invoicer
- [definition] primary\_recipients — An array of recipient objects. Each entry contains a `billing_info` object and optionally a `shipping_info` object. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#primary-recipients
- [definition] items — An array of line items on the invoice. Each item contains the following fields. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#items
- [definition] configuration — Options that control invoice behavior and payment rules. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#configuration
- [definition] amount — A read-only computed summary of the invoice total. PayPal populates this based on items and configuration. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#amount
- [definition] payments — A read-only record of payments made against the invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#payments
- [definition] refunds — A read-only record of refunds issued against the invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/invoice-object#refunds

## Invoice lifecycle and status flow
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle>

- [parameter] Draft: API status=`DRAFT`; Description and possible actions=All invoices start in draft status. The invoice is created but not sent. It is fully editable and invisible to the payer.; Next possible status=**Due**, **Scheduled**, **Overdue** (after the due date), **Marked as Paid**, or **Deleted** — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Due: API status=`SENT` or `UNPAID`; Description and possible actions=The invoice is payable. <br />- `SENT`: The invoice is sent to the customer’s registered email address through the invoicing UI. <br />- `UNPAID`: The invoice is shared through a link or QR code.; Next possible status=**Overdue** (after the due date), **Paid**, **Partially Paid**, or **Canceled** — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Overdue: API status=`SENT` or `UNPAID`; Description and possible actions=The invoice is payable and past due.<br />- `SENT`: The invoice is sent to the customer’s registered email address through the invoicing UI. <br />- `UNPAID`: The invoice is shared through a link or QR code.; Next possible status=**Paid**, **Partially Paid**, or **Canceled** — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Scheduled: API status=`SCHEDULED`; Description and possible actions=The invoice is scheduled for a future send date. The invoice is fully editable and invisible to the payer.; Next possible status=**Due** (on the scheduled date), **Overdue** (after the due date), or **Deleted** — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Partially Paid: API status=`PARTIALLY_PAID`; Description and possible actions=Partially paid with an outstanding balance.; Next possible status=**Paid** when the remaining balance is collected — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Paid: API status=`PAID` or `MARKED_AS_PAID`; Description and possible actions=- `PAID`: The invoice is fully paid and settled, and is no longer payable. <br />-`MARKED_AS_PAID`: The invoice is manually marked as paid to reflect an external payment for reconciliation.; Next possible status=**Partially Refunded**, **Marked as Refunded** or **Refunded**, if you issue a refund — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Payment Pending: API status=`PAYMENT_PENDING`; Description and possible actions=Payment is in progress but not yet completed.; Next possible status=**Paid**, **Partially Paid** — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Canceled: API status=`CANCELLED` or `AUTO_CANCELLED`; Description and possible actions=- `CANCELLED`: The invoice is canceled, no longer payable, and retained for audit and reference. <br />- `AUTO_CANCELLED`: The invoice is automatically cancelled.; Next possible status=No further transitions allowed — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Partially Refunded: API status=`PARTIALLY_REFUNDED`; Description and possible actions=The invoice has been partially refunded, so some but not all of the original payment has been returned.; Next possible status=No further transitions allowed — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [parameter] Refunded: API status=`REFUNDED` or `MARKED_AS_REFUNDED`; Description and possible actions=- `REFUNDED`: The invoice is fully refunded. <br />- `MARKED_AS_REFUNDED`: The invoice is manually marked as refunded to show that the refund was handled outside PayPal and is used for manual reconciliation.; Next possible status=No further transitions allowed — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses
- [definition] Invoice lifecycle and status flow — PayPal Invoicing defines a set of statuses that represent an invoice's lifecycle. Most invoices follow a simple flow: **Draft** → **Due** → **Paid** (or **Cancelled**). — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-lifecycle-and-status-flow
- [definition] Invoice statuses — The invoice status determines how the invoice should be interpreted and what actions are allowed at any given point. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/lifecycle#invoice-statuses

## Overview
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview>

- [parameter] Description: Invoicing=Use Invoicing to collect one-time or recurring payments from a specific customer; Payment Links=Use Payment Links to sell a product, service, or accept a donation or tip — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Customer: Invoicing=Specific individual or business; Payment Links=Anyone with the link — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Reusability: Invoicing=One invoice per transaction; Payment Links=Reuse the same link multiple times — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Integration options: Invoicing=Business Dashboard or API; Payment Links=Business Dashboard or API — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Sharing options: Invoicing=Email invoice, share hosted invoice link, or provide a QR code that opens the invoice; Payment Links=Share link through email, SMS, or social channels — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Customization: Invoicing=Invoice branding and business details.; Payment Links=Limited checkout customization — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Payment methods: Invoicing=PayPal, Pay Later, Venmo, Debit or Credit Cards, Apple Pay, and Pay by Bank (ACH); Payment Links=PayPal, cards, and eligible local methods — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Recurring payments: Invoicing=One-time invoices; Payment Links=One-time payments only — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Collection tools: Invoicing=Invoice reminders and status tracking; Payment Links=Not supported — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Customers can choose what to pay: Invoicing=❌; Payment Links=✅ — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Hosted payment page: Invoicing=✅; Payment Links=✅ — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Partial payments: Invoicing=✅; Payment Links=❌ — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Discounts: Invoicing=✅; Payment Links=✅ — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] Taxes: Invoicing=✅; Payment Links=✅ — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [parameter] [PCI compliance handling](https://www.paypal.com/us/brc/article/pci-dss-compliance-basics): Invoicing=✅; Payment Links=✅ — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link
- [definition] Overview — PayPal Invoicing lets merchants and partners create and send itemized invoices and get paid through a secure PayPal payment page, without building their own payment flow. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#overview
- [definition] Invoicing vs Payment Link — Use the following table to compare PayPal’s Invoicing and [Payment Links](/payments/pay-links-buttons), and choose the option that best matches your needs. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/overview#invoicing-vs-payment-link

## Quick start using Invoicing API
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api>

- [snippet] Step 1: Get your token: curl -X POST https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com/v1/oauth2/token \ — `curl -X POST https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com/v1/oauth2/token \` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#step-1-get-your-token
- [snippet] [expandable]: curl -X POST https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com/v2/invoicing/invoices \ — `curl -X POST https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com/v2/invoicing/invoices \` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#step-2-create-a-draft-invoice
- [snippet] Step 3: Send the invoice: curl -v -X POST https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com/v2/invoicing/invoices/{invoice_id}/send \ — `curl -v -X POST https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com/v2/invoicing/invoices/{invoice_id}/send \` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#step-3-send-the-invoice
- [definition] Quick start using Invoicing API — PayPal Invoicing API helps you create, update, send, and manage invoices programmatically from your backend systems. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#quick-start-using-invoicing-api
- [definition] What you'll build — After completing this quick start, you will have: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#what-youll-build
- [definition] Flow summary — 1. Your server sends a `POST` request to create a draft invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#flow-summary
- [definition] Step 1: Get your token — Open the dashboard, then create or select an app. Copy the **Client ID** and **Secret**. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#step-1-get-your-token
- [definition] Step 2: Create a draft invoice — Create a draft invoice in a single API call. The invoice is not visible to your customer yet. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#step-2-create-a-draft-invoice
- [definition] Step 3: Send the invoice — In your sandbox account, send the draft invoice you created in the previous step. This moves the invoice from `DRAFT` to `SENT` status, and PayPal sends a secure payment link in the email to your customer. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/quickstart-api#step-3-send-the-invoice

## Reference
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference>

- [definition] Reference — Use the PayPal Invoicing API to create, send, and manage invoices programmatically. This page covers all endpoints, request parameters, response objects, and error codes. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#reference
- [definition] Invoice endpoints — The following endpoints cover the full invoice lifecycle, from creating a draft to sending, updating, recording payments, and canceling. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#invoice-endpoints
- [definition] `POST` Create draft invoice — Creates a draft invoice. The customer isn't notified until you call `POST /v2/invoicing/invoices/{id}/send`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-create-draft-invoice
- [definition] `POST` Send invoice — Sends or schedules an invoice to be delivered to the customer, depending on the invoice issue date: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-send-invoice
- [definition] `GET` Show invoice details — Returns the full invoice object for a given invoice ID, including current status, line items, recipient information, payment and refund history, and HATEOAS links for available next actions. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#get-show-invoice-details
- [definition] `POST` Search for invoices — Searches for invoices matching multiple criteria simultaneously. All specified criteria must match. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-search-for-invoices
- [definition] `PUT` Fully update invoice — Fully replaces an invoice by ID. The request body must include the complete invoice object, and any fields omitted are cleared. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#put-fully-update-invoice
- [definition] `DELETE` Delete invoice — Permanently deletes a draft or scheduled invoice by ID. For sent invoices, use `POST /v2/invoicing/invoices/{id}/cancel` instead. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#delete-delete-invoice
- [definition] `POST` Cancel sent invoice — Cancels a sent invoice and changes its status to `CANCELLED`. For invoices that were never sent, use `DELETE /v2/invoicing/invoices/{id}` instead. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-cancel-sent-invoice
- [definition] `POST` Send invoice reminder — Sends a payment reminder email to the customer for an unpaid invoice. Use this to follow up on overdue invoices or prompt payment before the due date. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-send-invoice-reminder
- [definition] `POST` Record external payment — Records an offline payment against an invoice, for example, cash, check, bank transfer, or wire payment received outside of PayPal. PayPal updates the invoice status to `MARKED_AS_PAID` if the full balance is covered, or `PARTIALLY_PAID` if a partial amount is recorded. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-record-external-payment
- [definition] `DELETE` Delete external payment — Deletes a previously recorded offline payment from an invoice. Use this to correct a payment entry recorded in error via `POST /v2/invoicing/invoices/{id}/payments`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#delete-delete-external-payment
- [definition] `POST` Record refund for invoice — Records an offline refund against a paid invoice, for example, a refund issued through cash, check, or bank transfer outside of PayPal. Updates the invoice status to `MARKED_AS_REFUNDED` if fully refunded, or `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED` if a partial amount is recorded. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-record-refund-for-invoice
- [definition] `DELETE` Delete external refund — Deletes a previously recorded offline refund from an invoice. Use this to correct a refund entry recorded in error through `POST /v2/invoicing/invoices/{id}/refunds`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#delete-delete-external-refund
- [definition] `POST` Generate invoice number — Generates the next sequential invoice number for the authenticated merchant, incrementing from the last used number and preserving any prefix or suffix pattern. For example, the next number after `INVOICE-1234` is `INVOICE-1235`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-generate-invoice-number
- [definition] `POST` Generate QR code — Generates a Base64-encoded PNG QR code for a sent invoice. Add it to a paper or PDF invoice — when a customer scans it with a mobile device they are redirected to a PayPal-hosted payment page to pay using PayPal or card. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-generate-qr-code
- [definition] Template endpoints — The following endpoints let you create and manage reusable invoice templates. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#template-endpoints
- [definition] `POST` Create template — Creates a reusable invoice template that pre-populates fields — such as merchant information, line items, payment terms, and tax configuration — when creating new invoices. Templates reduce repetitive data entry for merchants who regularly send the same invoice type. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#post-create-template
- [definition] `GET` List templates — Returns a list of merchant-created invoice templates, including associated business information such as emails, addresses, and phone numbers from the merchant's PayPal profile. Use the returned `template_id` when creating invoices to pre-populate fields from a saved configuration. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#get-list-templates
- [definition] `GET` Show template details — Returns the full template object for a given template ID, including all configured fields, display settings, line item defaults, and payment configuration. Retrieve this before a full update to avoid unintentionally clearing fields. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#get-show-template-details
- [definition] `PUT` Fully update template — Fully replaces an invoice template by ID. The request body must include the complete template object — fields omitted will be cleared. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#put-fully-update-template
- [definition] `DELETE` Delete template — Permanently deletes an invoice template by ID. Existing invoices created from this template aren't affected. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/reference#delete-delete-template

## Templates
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates>

- [snippet] Request: curl -v -X POST https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com/v2/invoicing/templates \ — `curl -v -X POST https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com/v2/invoicing/templates \` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#request
- [snippet] Response: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#response
- [parameter] `POST`: Endpoint=`/v2/invoicing/templates`; Description=Create a new template. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#templates-api
- [parameter] `GET`: Endpoint=`/v2/invoicing/templates`; Description=List all templates. Use `?fields=all` for full details or `?fields=none` for name, ID, and default flag only. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#templates-api
- [parameter] `GET`: Endpoint=`/v2/invoicing/templates/{template_id}`; Description=Show details for a single template. Use `@default` as the ID to retrieve the current default template. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#templates-api
- [parameter] `PUT`: Endpoint=`/v2/invoicing/templates/{template_id}`; Description=Fully update a template by ID. Doesn't support partial updates, so you'll need to include the complete template object. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#templates-api
- [parameter] `DELETE`: Endpoint=`/v2/invoicing/templates/{template_id}`; Description=Delete a template by ID. Returns `HTTP 204 No Content`. Can't be used on PayPal system templates where `standard_template` is `true`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#templates-api
- [parameter] `id`: Type=string; Required=Read-only; Description=The PayPal-generated template ID (`TEMP-XXXXXXXXXXXX`). — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [parameter] `name`: Type=string; Required=Required; Description=A merchant-defined name for the template. Shown in the dashboard and template list. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [parameter] `default_template`: Type=boolean; Required=Optional; Description=If `true`, this template is used as the default when creating invoices. Only one template can be the default at a time. Default: `false`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [parameter] `template_info`: Type=object; Required=Optional; Description=The invoice details are pre-populated by this template. See [**template\_info**](#template-info) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [parameter] `settings`: Type=array; Required=Optional; Description=Display rules that control which fields are shown or hidden on invoices created from this template. See [**settings**](#settings) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [parameter] `unit_of_measure`: Type=string; Required=Optional; Description=The unit of measure for line items. Values: `QUANTITY`, `HOURS`, `AMOUNT`. Default: `QUANTITY`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [parameter] `standard_template`: Type=boolean; Required=Read-only; Description=If `true`, this is a PayPal system template and can't be deleted. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [parameter] `links`: Type=array; Required=Read-only; Description=HATEOAS links for available actions on the template, for example, `self`, `delete`, `replace`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [parameter] `detail`: Type=object; Description=Default invoice detail including `currency_code`, `note`, `terms_and_conditions`, `memo`, `reference`, and `attachments`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-info
- [parameter] `invoicer`: Type=object; Description=The merchant's business information, including name, address, email, phone, logo URL, and tax ID. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-info
- [parameter] `primary_recipients`: Type=array; Description=Default recipient billing and shipping information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-info
- [parameter] `items`: Type=array; Description=Default line items with name, quantity, unit amount, tax, and unit of measure. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-info
- [parameter] `configuration`: Type=object; Description=Default invoice configuration including `allow_tip`, `tax_calculated_after_discount`, `tax_inclusive`, and `partial_payment` settings. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-info
- [parameter] `amount`: Type=object; Description=Default amount breakdown including shipping, custom charges, and discount. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-info
- [parameter] `items.date`: The date column on line items. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#settings
- [parameter] `items.description`: The description column on line items. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#settings
- [parameter] `items.discount`: The per-item discount field. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#settings
- [parameter] `items.tax`: The per-item tax field. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#settings
- [parameter] `discount`: The invoice-level discount field. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#settings
- [parameter] `shipping`: The shipping charge field. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#settings
- [parameter] `custom`: The custom charge field, for example, packing fees. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#settings
- [definition] Templates — Templates let you save reusable invoice details such as your business information, standard line items, payment terms, and display preferences so you don't have to repeat them every time you create an invoice. You can create up to 50 templates per merchant account. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#templates
- [definition] Templates API — Templates are managed through five endpoints covering the full create, read, update, and delete lifecycle. Use `POST` to create a new template, `GET` to retrieve either a single template by ID or your full list, `PUT` to fully update an existing template, and `DELETE` to remove one. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#templates-api
- [definition] Template object — The following fields make up the template object. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-object
- [definition] template\_info — The invoice data stored in the template shares the same structure as the invoice object. See the most commonly used fields. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#template-info
- [definition] settings — An array of display rules controlling field visibility on invoices created from this template. Each entry contains a `field_name` and a `display_preference` object with a single `hidden` boolean. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#settings
- [definition] Create a template — Creates a new invoice template. A successful request returns `HTTP 201 Created` and the template object if you set `Prefer: return=representation`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/templates#create-a-template
