## Troubleshooting Invoicing API
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting>

- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#error-response-format
- [parameter] `400`: Error name=`INVALID_REQUEST`; Message=Invalid request; Details=One or more validation errors have occurred. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#api-errors
- [parameter] `401`: Error name=`AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE`; Message=Authorization error occurred; Details=Authorization error occurred. Check your credentials. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#api-errors
- [parameter] `404`: Error name=`RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND`; Message=Resource not found; Details=The resource requested is not found in the system. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#api-errors
- [parameter] `422`: Error name=`UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY`; Message=Invoicing business error; Details=A detailed error message is passed in the message field. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#api-errors
- [parameter] `429`: Error name=`RATE_LIMIT_REACHED`; Message=Too many requests and user is blocked due to rate limiting; Details=The rate limit for the user, application, or token exceeds a predefined value. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#api-errors
- [parameter] `500`: Error name=`INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR`; Message=Internal service error; Details=Resend the request at another time. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#api-errors
- [parameter] `403`: Issue=`NOT_AUTHORIZED`; Message=Authorization failed due to insufficient permissions; Common cause=Invalid access token or account permissions; Affected fields=`invoiceId`, `templateId` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#authorization-errors
- [parameter] `403`: Issue=`PERMISSION_DENIED`; Message=The requested invoice and template are not associated with the requested user; Common cause=Accessing resources that don't belong to your account; Affected fields=`invoiceId`, `templateId` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#authorization-errors
- [parameter] `INVALID_STRING_LENGTH`: Description=Currency code must be 3 characters; Affected fields=`/detail/currency_code`, `/amount/currency_code` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#request-validation-errors
- [parameter] `INVALID_STRING_MAX_LENGTH`: Description=Field exceeds maximum character limit; Affected fields=`/detail/note`, `/items/0/name`, `/detail/invoice_number` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#request-validation-errors
- [parameter] `INVALID_PARAMETER_SYNTAX`: Description=Invalid format of the dates, phone numbers, or country codes; Affected fields=`/detail/invoice_date`, `/detail/payment_term/due_date` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#request-validation-errors
- [parameter] `MISSING_REQUIRED_PARAMETER`: Description=Required field is missing; Affected fields=`/method`, `/amount/value` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#request-validation-errors
- [parameter] `INVOICE_ALREADY_SENT`: The system blocks sending any invoice that has already been sent. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#business-logic-errors
- [parameter] `CANT_SEND_INVOICE_WITHOUT_EMAIL`: A recipient email address is required before an invoice can be sent. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#business-logic-errors
- [parameter] `CANT_CANCEL_INVOICE_IN_DRAFT_STATE`: Cannot cancel an invoice that is in draft state. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#business-logic-errors
- [parameter] `CANT_PAY_AN_PAID_OR_CANCELED_INVOICE`: Cannot record a payment on an invoice that is already paid or canceled. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#business-logic-errors
- [parameter] `PAYMENT_AMOUNT_GREATER_THAN_AMOUNT_DUE`: Payment amount cannot exceed the total amount due on the invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#business-logic-errors
- [parameter] `CANT_REFUND_MORE_THAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT`: Cannot refund more than the amount of recorded payments. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#business-logic-errors
- [parameter] `INVOICE_CANNOT_BE_DELETED`: Only draft, scheduled, or canceled invoices can be deleted. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#business-logic-errors
- [parameter] `INVOICE_NOT_FOUND`: Invoice with specified id does not exists. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#business-logic-errors
- [parameter] Invoice number: 25 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Currency code: 3 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Country code: 2 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Email address: 3–254 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Phone number: 1–14 digits — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Item name: 200 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Item description: 1000 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Invoice note: 4000 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Terms and conditions: 4000 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Reference field: 120 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Logo URL: 2000 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [parameter] Website URL: 2048 — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#field-validation-limits
- [definition] Troubleshooting Invoicing API — The PayPal Invoicing API returns specific error codes and messages to help you troubleshoot integration issues. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#troubleshooting-invoicing-api
- [definition] Error response format — All Invoicing API errors follow this structure: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#error-response-format
- [definition] API errors — PayPal returns HTTP `4XX` status codes if something passes in the request has an error. `5XX` status codes appear when something is wrong on our end with a server or service. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/troubleshooting#api-errors

## Use cases
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases>

- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-draft-invoice-with-minimum-required-fields
- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-payment-request-linked-to-an-external-invoice
- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-payment-request-linked-to-an-external-invoice
- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-digital-goods
- [snippet] [expandable]: { — `{` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice
- [parameter] `detail.invoice_number`: Your reference number for the invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-draft-invoice-with-minimum-required-fields
- [parameter] `detail.currency_code`: Currency applied to all monetary values. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-draft-invoice-with-minimum-required-fields
- [parameter] `detail.payment_term.term_type`: When payment is due, such as `DUE_ON_RECEIPT`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-draft-invoice-with-minimum-required-fields
- [parameter] `invoicer`: Sender name and email. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-draft-invoice-with-minimum-required-fields
- [parameter] `primary_recipients`: Customer name and email. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-draft-invoice-with-minimum-required-fields
- [parameter] `items`: At least one line item with name, quantity, and unit amount. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-draft-invoice-with-minimum-required-fields
- [parameter] `items[].unit_of_measure: "AMOUNT"`: Flat-rate billing with no quantity or hourly breakdown. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-payment-request-linked-to-an-external-invoice
- [parameter] `attachments`: Attach the original invoice PDF for the customer's reference. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-payment-request-linked-to-an-external-invoice
- [parameter] `items[].unit_of_measure: "HOURS"`: Renders quantity as hours worked and `unit_amount` as the hourly rate. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [parameter] `primary_recipients[].billing_info.address`: Client billing address; no `shipping_info` included. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [parameter] `detail.payment_term.term_type`: When payment is due, such as `NET_14` or `NET_30`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [parameter] `detail.note`: Project summary or message visible to the client. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [parameter] `detail.term`: Formal payment terms and conditions. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [parameter] `configuration.allow_tip: true`: Enables an optional tip or gratuity field on the PayPal-hosted payment page. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [parameter] `configuration.partial_payment`: Supports deposit or installment payment workflows. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [parameter] `primary_recipients[].shipping_info`: Buyer's shipping address. This can differ from billing address. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [parameter] `primary_recipients[].billing_info`: Buyer's billing address. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [parameter] `items[].unit_of_measure: "QUANTITY"`: Unit-based billing for physical goods. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [parameter] `amount.breakdown.shipping.amount`: Flat shipping charge. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [parameter] `amount.breakdown.shipping.tax`: Tax applied specifically to the shipping charge. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [parameter] `items[].tax`: Per-line sales tax on individual goods. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [parameter] `detail.reference`: Purchase order or order reference number. This value is customer-visible. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [parameter] `items[].unit_of_measure: "QUANTITY"`: Unit-based billing for seat or license-based products. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-digital-goods
- [parameter] `items[].unit_of_measure: "AMOUNT"`: Flat-rate billing for bundles or one-time packages. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-digital-goods
- [parameter] `primary_recipients[].billing_info`: Billing address only; no `shipping_info` included. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-digital-goods
- [parameter] `items[].tax`: Applicable sales tax. Digital goods tax rules vary by jurisdiction. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-digital-goods
- [parameter] `detail.note`: Delivery instructions or next-step guidance visible to the customer after payment. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-digital-goods
- [parameter] `items[].unit_of_measure: "HOURS"`: Billable time; quantity = hours worked, `unit_amount` = hourly rate. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice
- [parameter] `items[].unit_of_measure: "QUANTITY"`: Discrete units; quantity = number of items, `unit_amount` = unit price. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice
- [parameter] `items[].unit_of_measure: "AMOUNT"`: Flat-rate charge; quantity = `"1"`, `unit_amount` = total flat fee. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice
- [parameter] `items[].discount`: Line item-level discount, such as percent or fixed amount. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice
- [parameter] `amount.breakdown.discount`: Invoice-level discount applied across all line items. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice
- [parameter] `configuration.partial_payment`: Supports structured deposit and balance workflows. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice
- [parameter] `detail.memo`: Private internal note, not visible to the client. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice
- [definition] Use cases — Explore key ways to apply the PayPal Invoicing API in your billing and payment collection flows. See how different configuration options map to common business scenarios so you can choose the pattern that best fits your integration. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#use-cases
- [definition] Use cases — See how to create invoices that address common scenarios and requirements. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#use-cases
- [definition] Create a draft invoice with minimum required fields — This is a good starting point for merchants new to the API or developers testing an integration before adding complexity. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-draft-invoice-with-minimum-required-fields
- [definition] Create a payment request linked to an external invoice — This pattern is ideal for merchants using a enterprise resource planning (ERP) or accounting platforms like NetSuite, QuickBooks, or Xero who want to collect payment through PayPal without recreating the invoice. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-payment-request-linked-to-an-external-invoice
- [definition] Create an hourly services invoice — Create an hourly services invoice with `unit_of_measure: "HOURS"`, a client billing address, tip enabled, and partial payment configured for deposit workflows. This pattern works best for consultants, freelancers, photographers, and tradespeople billing for time and labor. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-hourly-services-invoice
- [definition] Create an invoice for shippable goods — Create a physical goods invoice with quantity-based line items, separate billing and shipping addresses, a shipping charge, and shipping-specific tax. This pattern works best for e-commerce sellers, wholesalers, and distributors shipping physical products. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-shippable-goods
- [definition] Create an invoice for digital goods — Create a digital goods invoice with unit and flat-rate line items and a billing address only — no shipping address or charges. This pattern works best for merchants selling software licenses, downloads, online courses, or other digital products. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-an-invoice-for-digital-goods
- [definition] Create a mixed billing invoice — Create an invoice that combines all three `unit_of_measure` types — `HOURS`, `QUANTITY`, and `AMOUNT` — in a single request, with an invoice-level discount and partial payment configuration. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-cases#create-a-mixed-billing-invoice

## Manage your domain
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain>

- [parameter] Domain verified: Activate your domain from Invoicing settings. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#ongoing-domain-monitoring
- [parameter] Verification failed: Review and correct your DNS records, then re-verify from Invoicing settings. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#ongoing-domain-monitoring
- [parameter] Transient error reverted to [service@paypal.com](mailto:service@paypal.com): No action required. PayPal will attempt to re-verify automatically. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#ongoing-domain-monitoring
- [parameter] Transient error resolved and domain verified: Activate your domain from Invoicing settings. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#ongoing-domain-monitoring
- [definition] Manage your domain — Manage your verified custom email domain after you complete the initial setup. Before you begin, [Set up your domain](/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain). — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#manage-your-domain
- [definition] Update your sender email prefix — You can update your sender email prefix at any time without re-verifying your domain. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#update-your-sender-email-prefix
- [definition] Deactivate your domain — Deactivating your domain reverts all invoice emails to [service@paypal.com](mailto:service@paypal.com) until you reactivate it. Your domain remains verified and you can reactivate it at any time. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#deactivate-your-domain
- [definition] Reactivate your domain — 1. Go to **Invoicing** > **Settings** > **Domain**. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#reactivate-your-domain
- [definition] Remove your domain — Removing your domain permanently reverts all invoice emails to [service@paypal.com](mailto:service@paypal.com). To use a custom domain again, complete the setup wizard from the beginning. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#remove-your-domain
- [definition] Ongoing domain monitoring — After initial verification, PayPal continuously monitors your domain to ensure email deliverability is maintained. If a record goes missing or becomes invalid, custom sending is suspended and emails revert to [service@paypal.com](mailto:service@paypal.com) until the records are restored. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#ongoing-domain-monitoring
- [definition] Switch to a different domain — Each merchant account supports one custom domain at a time. To switch to a new domain, remove your existing domain first, then run through the dashboard setup again. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/manage-domain#switch-to-a-different-domain

## Overview
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/overview>

Set up your custom email in your [PayPal dashboard](https://www.paypal.com/invoice/s/settings/custom-domain?utm_medium=paypal_dev_docs) without the need for API integration. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/overview#overview

## Set up your domain
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain>

- [snippet] Step 2: Configure DNS records: TXT  _dmarc  v=DMARC1; p=none; rua=mailto:dmarc@acme.com — `TXT  _dmarc  v=DMARC1; p=none; rua=mailto:dmarc@acme.com` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-2-configure-dns-records
- [parameter] Type: A, AAAA, or CNAME depending on your setup — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#create-a-subdomain
- [parameter] Name / Host: Your desired subdomain prefix, such as `api`, `staging`, or `docs` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#create-a-subdomain
- [parameter] Value / Points to: The IP address or hostname of the server handling the subdomain — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#create-a-subdomain
- [parameter] TTL: `300` (5 min) for testing or `3600` for production — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#create-a-subdomain
- [parameter] Sender Policy Framework (SPF): Type=TXT; Purpose=Authorizes mail servers to send email on behalf of your domain — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-2-configure-dns-records
- [parameter] DomainKeys Identified Mail 1 (DKIM): Type=CNAME; Purpose=Adds a digital signature to emails so recipients can verify they weren't tampered with — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-2-configure-dns-records
- [parameter] DKIM 2: Type=CNAME; Purpose=Provides a backup DKIM key for continued email authentication — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-2-configure-dns-records
- [parameter] `v` (required): Description=Protocol version. Must be `DMARC1`; Example=`v=DMARC1` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-2-configure-dns-records
- [parameter] `p` (required): Description=Domain policy. Accepted values: `none`, `quarantine`, `reject`; Example=`p=none` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-2-configure-dns-records
- [parameter] `rua` (optional): Description=Address to receive aggregate DMARC reports; Example=`rua=mailto:dmarc@acme.com` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-2-configure-dns-records
- [parameter] Setup incomplete: Domain registered is awaiting DNS configuration and verification. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#verification-status
- [parameter] Verifying: Verification in progress can take up to 72 hours. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#verification-status
- [parameter] Verified: All DNS records confirmed. Activate domain. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#verification-status
- [parameter] Error: One or more checks failed. Review per-record status, correct the records, and verify again. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#verification-status
- [definition] Set up your domain — Send PayPal invoice emails from your own domain instead of a paypal.com address. This guide shows you how to create a subdomain, configure the required DNS records, and verify your domain with PayPal. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#set-up-your-domain
- [definition] Create a subdomain — A subdomain is a prefix added to your domain. It is configured through DNS records, not purchased separately. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#create-a-subdomain
- [definition] Step 1: Add your subdomain — 1. In the PayPal dashboard, select **Invoicing** > **Settings** > **Domain**. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-1-add-your-subdomain
- [definition] Step 2: Configure DNS records — After you submit your domain, PayPal displays the required DNS records on the DNS configuration page. Add all three records to your DNS provider before starting the verification process. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-2-configure-dns-records
- [definition] Step 3: Verify and activate — Once you've added all the DNS records, return to your PayPal dashboard to verify ownership and activate your domain. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#step-3-verify-and-activate
- [definition] Verification status — After selecting **Verify Domain**, your domain enters one of the following states: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#verification-status
- [definition] After activation — When your domain is active, all buyer-facing invoice emails are sent from your custom sender address automatically. No additional configuration is required. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/set-up-domain#after-activation

## Troubleshooting
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting>

- [parameter] SPF, DKIM, or DMARC failure: PayPal automatically attempts to re-verify your domain and retry sending. If your domain is no longer valid, the email falls back to [service@paypal.com](mailto:service@paypal.com). See [High bounce rates](/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#high-bounce-rates) for more information. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#bounce-handling
- [parameter] Invalid or nonexistent recipient address: PayPal sends a bounce notification to the merchant. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#bounce-handling
- [parameter] Recipient mailbox full: PayPal sends a bounce notification to the merchant. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#bounce-handling
- [definition] Troubleshooting — Resolve verification failures, bounce issues, and DNS configuration errors. If you run into issues with your custom email domain, use this page to diagnose and resolve common problems. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#troubleshooting
- [definition] Domain not verifying — If your domain hasn't verified after 72 hours, check the following: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#domain-not-verifying
- [definition] Emails reverting to [service@paypal.com](mailto:service@paypal.com) — PayPal sends your emails from [service@paypal.com](mailto:service@paypal.com) instead of your custom domain when a domain health check fails. Common causes: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#emails-reverting-to-servicepaypalcommailtoservicepaypalcom
- [definition] Bounce handling — If a custom-domain email bounces, PayPal's behavior depends on the cause: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#bounce-handling
- [definition] High bounce rates — A `5.7.x` bounce code indicates a domain authentication failure. Check that your SPF, DKIM, and DMARC records are correctly configured using [dnschecker.org](https://dnschecker.org) or select **Verify Domain** from Invoicing Settings. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/use-your-email-domain/troubleshooting#high-bounce-rates

- [parameter] `INVOICING.INVOICE.REFUNDED`: PayPal sends this webhook when=A merchant issues a full or partial refund for an invoice; API reference=[Mark invoice as refunded](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/invoicing/v1/#invoice_record-refund) — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/webhooks#invoicing-webhooks
- [parameter] `INVOICING.INVOICE.SCHEDULED`: PayPal sends this webhook when=A merchant schedules an invoice; API reference=[Schedule invoice](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/invoicing/v1/#invoice_schedule) — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/webhooks#invoicing-webhooks
- [parameter] `INVOICING.INVOICE.UPDATED`: PayPal sends this webhook when=A merchant updates an invoice; API reference=[Update invoice](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/invoicing/v1/#invoice_update) — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/webhooks#invoicing-webhooks
- [definition] Invoicing webhooks — When a merchant creates, updates, pays, or cancels an invoice, PayPal sends a webhook event to your server. Your server can then process the events automatically. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/grow-business/invoicing/webhooks#invoicing-webhooks

## Countries and supported features
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features>

- [parameter] Send, receive, and withdraw: Merchants can send payouts from this country. PayPal account holders can receive and withdraw money. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#feature-sets-and-what-they-mean
- [parameter] Send, receive, and withdraw in local currency: Merchants can send payouts from this country. PayPal account holders can receive and withdraw money in local currency. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#feature-sets-and-what-they-mean
- [parameter] Fully localized: Merchants can send payouts from this country. PayPal account holders can receive and withdraw money in local currency. The PayPal app is available in the local language. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#feature-sets-and-what-they-mean
- [parameter] Receive and withdraw: Merchants cannot send payouts from this country. PayPal account holders can receive and withdraw money. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#feature-sets-and-what-they-mean
- [parameter] Andorra: Feature set=Send, receive, and withdraw; Country Code=`AD` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#eligible-countries-and-their-feature-sets

- [definition] Countries and supported features — You can use the following information to review country and currency requirements before sending payouts. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#countries-and-supported-features
- [definition] Feature sets and what they mean — The following table summarizes the payout feature sets: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#feature-sets-and-what-they-mean
- [definition] Eligible countries and their feature sets — The following table lists countries eligible for payouts and their available feature sets: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#eligible-countries-and-their-feature-sets
- [definition] Country-specific payout restrictions — The following table lists countries with specific payout restrictions: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#country-specific-payout-restrictions
- [definition] Currency conversion — PayPal Payouts supports automatic currency conversion when the payout currency differs from the recipient’s account currency. However, some countries and currencies have exclusions or restrictions. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#currency-conversion
- [definition] Exclusions — Currency conversion is not available for payouts to the following countries and regions: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#exclusions
- [definition] Restrictions — The following table lists the countries that have specific restrictions on currency conversion for payouts: — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#restrictions
- [definition] Supported currencies for automatic conversion — When you send a payout in a currency different from the recipient’s account currency, PayPal automatically converts the amount using the applicable exchange rate, which includes PayPal’s currency conversion spread or fee. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/reference/countries-supported-features#supported-currencies-for-automatic-conversion

## Use large-batch file transfer
<https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer>

- [snippet] Sample summary line: PAYOUT_SUMMARY,1500.75,USD,5,"Thank you, ""Top Seller""!",Payout for May — `PAYOUT_SUMMARY,1500.75,USD,5,"Thank you, ""Top Seller""!",Payout for May` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#summary-line-format
- [snippet] Sample payout line items: PAYOUT,test@paypal.com,1000.50,USD,REF_ID_1,Thanks for your work — `PAYOUT,test@paypal.com,1000.50,USD,REF_ID_1,Thanks for your work` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#payout-item-row-format
- [snippet] Example: PAYOUT_SUMMARY,USD,INVALID_CURRENCY,Invalid currency code in line 3 — `PAYOUT_SUMMARY,USD,INVALID_CURRENCY,Invalid currency code in line 3` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#acknowledgment-reports
- [snippet] Example: PAYOUT,3,REF_ID_1,INVALID_CURRENCY,Invalid currency code — `PAYOUT,3,REF_ID_1,INVALID_CURRENCY,Invalid currency code` — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#acknowledgment-reports

- [parameter] Currency <br /><span>Required</span>: Description=Three-letter ISO 4217 currency code. - [parameter] Accepted (**ACK**): <span>File name suffix</span>=`_ack.csv`; <span>What it means</span>=File is ready for processing.; <span>What to do</span>=Wait for PayPal to process your payouts. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#acknowledgment-reports
- [parameter] Not Accepted (**NACK**): <span>File name suffix</span>=`_nack.csv`; <span>What it means</span>=File has errors.; <span>What to do</span>=Fix the errors and upload a new file with a different name. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#acknowledgment-reports
- [parameter] Duplicate (**DUPS**): <span>File name suffix</span>=`_dups.csv`; <span>What it means</span>=File name already used.; <span>What to do</span>=Change the file name and upload again. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#acknowledgment-reports
- [parameter] Part File report: <span>When you get it</span>=After part of your file is processed.; <span>What it shows</span>=Shows results for the processed part. Large files may have multiple part files as PayPal processes them in sections.; <span>What to do</span>=Check each part file to track progress and fix problems as they appear. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#3-review-stage-reports
- [parameter] Out or Interim report: <span>When you get it</span>=After your whole file is processed.; <span>What it shows</span>=Shows results for all payments in your file, including status and errors.; <span>What to do</span>=Use this to check your records and confirm all payments were sent. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#3-review-stage-reports
- [parameter] Final report: <span>When you get it</span>=31 days after the Interim report.; <span>What it shows</span>=Shows the final status of payments that were not claimed.; <span>What to do</span>=Check which payments were not claimed and decide what to do next. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#3-review-stage-reports

- [parameter] File not empty: <span>What PayPal checks</span>=Checks if your file has content and is not broken.; <span>Result</span>=`NACK` if empty or broken — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-steps-and-outcomes
- [parameter] File encoding: <span>What PayPal checks</span>=Makes sure your file uses UTF-8 text format.; <span>Result</span>=`NACK` if wrong format — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-steps-and-outcomes

- [parameter] Duplicate file: <span>What PayPal checks</span>=Checks if you already sent a file with this name.; <span>Result</span>=`DUPS` if already used — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-steps-and-outcomes
- [parameter] Sandbox limit: <span>What PayPal checks</span>=Checks if you have too many items (sandbox only).; <span>Result</span>=`NACK` if too many — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-steps-and-outcomes

Remove these characters, columns, or details and upload the file. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `INVALID_FILE_NAME`: The format of the file name is invalid. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `INVALID_FIRST_COLUMN`: The first column entry in the summary must be `PAYOUT_SUMMARY`. For an item record, this must be `PAYOUT`. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `INVALID_REF_ID_FORMAT`: The reference ID must be alphanumeric. The maximum character limit is 63. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `INVALID_SUMMARY_LINE_POSITION`: The payout summary record is missing from the first line in the payout input file. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors

- [parameter] `MULTIPLE_SUMMARY_RECORDS`: The payout input file should contain only one summary record. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `PAYOUT_AMOUNT_INVALID_FORMAT`: The payout amount must use a decimal. Other special characters are not allowed. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `PAYOUT_AMOUNT_NON_POSITIVE`: The payout amount must be greater than zero. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `SANDBOX_LIMIT_ERROR`: The number of items in the file exceeds the sandbox file limit (sandbox only). — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `SCHEDULED_TIME_ERROR`: The payout can be scheduled a maximum of seven days in advance. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `SUMMARY_AMOUNT_INVALID_FORMAT`: The summary amount must be a decimal. Other special characters are not allowed. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `SUMMARY_AMOUNT_NON_POSITIVE`: The total payout amount in the summary line must be greater than zero. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `SUMMARY_AND_PAYOUT_MATCH_CONFLICT`: The payout amount in the summary line does not match the total item value. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `SUMMARY_LINES_NON_INTEGER`: The total number of payments field in the summary line must be an integer. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `SUMMARY_LINES_NON_POSITIVE`: The total number of payments field in summary line must be greater than zero. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `SUMMARY_MISSING`: The summary line is missing from the payout input file. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `TOTAL_PAYMENTS_MISMATCH`: The `TOTAL_NO_OF_PAYMENTS` in the `PAYOUTS_SUMMARY` row must match the total records in the payout input file. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [parameter] `INVALID_PURPOSE`: The purpose specified for the transaction is invalid. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#file-validation-errors
- [definition] Use large-batch file transfer — You can use this pattern to pay more than 15,000 people at once with a single payment file. Common use-cases for this functionality include payroll payouts, rebate payouts, reward payouts, and affiliate program payouts. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#use-large-batch-file-transfer
- [definition] Send payouts — You can use the procedures in this section to send payouts using the large-batch file transfer. — https://docs.paypal.ai/growth/payouts/send-money/use-large-batch-file-transfer#send-payouts
